getMechQueen() : null;\n  // 白天/玩家死:上飞离场(原版 :26585/:27208 vy-=0.04 + EncourageDespawn(10))——\n  // 玩家死亡派发为 null,一并进离场（此前 null 仅漂移不消散）\n  if (game.world.clock.isDay || !player || player.dead) {\n    e.vy -= 0.04;\n    e.encourageDespawn(10); // 离屏即消(Enemy 打 bossFled,Game 不播击败/不记账)\n    e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;\n    return;\n  }\n  // reflectsProjectiles 每帧复位(:26582/:27204)——mech 变身段(:26812/:27435)置真\n  e.reflectsProjectiles = false;\n  // ---- 旋转瞄准(num427/num471:atan2(dy-59, 自-玩)+π/2,步进 0.1/0.15) ----\n  let target = Math.atan2(e.y + e.h - 59 - (player.y + player.h / 2), e.cx - player.cx) + Math.PI / 2;\n  if (target < 0) target += 6.283; else if (target > 6.283) target -= 6.283;\n  // :27135-27139 魔眼 mech 态悬浮(三阶段 ai0==3 && ai1==0)旋转步进 ×0.25\n  //  (锚位悬浮时头缓慢转向玩家)\n  let step = spaz ? ```ts
export function skeletronPrimeAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
  // 初始化:生成 128/129/130/131(ai0=侧 ±1, ai1=头 id;130/131 ai3=150 预热)
  if (!e.bInit) {
    e.bInit = true;
    const specs: Array<[number, number]> = [[128, -1], [129, 1], [130, -1], [131, 1]];
    for (const [id, side] of specs) {
      const p = spawnPart(game, e, id);
      if (p) { p.ai0 = side; p.ai1 = e.id; p.ai3 = id === 130 || id === 131 ? 150 : 0; p.bInit = true; }
    }
  }
  // 每 tick 从基值重置(vanilla damage=defDamage/defense=defDefense 置顶)
  const b = baseOf(e);
  e.def.damage = b.dmg;
  e.def.defense = b.def;
  // 玩家死亡(=无有效目标):按离场处理(原版 TargetClosest 落空 → ai[1]=3)
  if (!player) e.ai1 = 3;
  // 玩家死/超 6000px → 离场
  if (!player || Math.abs(e.cx - player.cx) > 6000 || Math.abs(e.cy - player.cy) > 6000) e.ai1 = 3;
  if (!player) { e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy; return; }
  // 白天 → 狂暴...
  if (game.world.clock.isDay && e.ai1 !== 3 && e.ai1 !== 2) {
    e.ai3 = player && player.cx > e.cx ? 1 : -1;
    e.ai1 = 2;
    game.playSfx('roar', 0.8);
  }
  if (e.ai1 === 0) {
    e.ai2 += 1;
    if (e.ai2 >= 600) { e.ai2 = 0; e.ai1 = 1; e.ai3 = player.cx > e.cx ? 1 : -1; }
    const tilt = (e.vx / 15) * 0.5;
    e.spin += (tilt - e.spin) * 0.75;
    ... Y band 200/500, X window ±100 ...
  } else if (e.ai1 === 1) {
    ... charge: def×2, spin += ai3*0.3, spd 2 ...
  } else if (e.ai1 === 2) {
    ... enrage ...
  } else {
    ... leave: vy += 0.1 ...
  }
  e.facing = ...;
  e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
}
```

150 : 0; p.bInit = true; }\n    }\n  }\n  // AI_032 :27738-27744:伤害/防御基值复位 + queen 每 tick 重登记(ai[3]!=0 →\n  // mechQueen=whoAmI 的本仓等价 = WeakSet 标记,见 mechQueen.ts 头注)+ 反弹复位\n  const b = baseOf(e);\n  e.def.damage = b.dmg;\n  e.def.defense = b.def;\n  registerMechQueen(e);\n  e.reflectsProjectiles = false;\n  const mq = mechQueenUp();\n  // 玩家死亡(=无有效目标):按离场处理(原版 TargetClosest 落空 → ai[1]=3)\n  if (!player) e.ai1 = 3;\n  // 玩家死/超 6000px → 离场\n  if (!player || Math.abs(e.cx - player.cx) > 6000 || Math.abs(e.cy - player.cy) > 6000) e.ai1 = 3;\n  // mech 离场级联(:27975-28011)——玩家死亡路径同样要跑,先于 !player 早退:\n  //  先 EncourageDespawn(5) 双子;双子尽 → 毁灭者头 Transform(136)(ai[1]=0 →\n  //  AI_037 段死分支 :50189-50202 静默消散,136 无掉落/不置 downed)+ 自身消散\n  if (e.ai1 === 3 && mq) {\n    const ens = game.enemies() as Enemy[];\n    for (const t of ens) {\n      if (!t.dead && (t.vanillaId === 125 || t.vanillaId === 126)) t.encourageDespawn(5);\n    }\n    if (!ens.some((t) => !t.dead && (t.vanillaId === 125 || t.vanillaId === 126))) {\n      const head = ens.find((t) => !t.dead && t.vanillaId === 134);\n      if (head) { head.bossFled = true; head.dead = true; }  // Transform(136) 等价消散\n      e.encourageDespawn(5);\n    }\n  }\n  if (!player) { e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy; return; }\n  // 白天 → 狂暴(原版不逃走,持续 9999 追杀;Game.ts 日出剔除对 127 豁免)\n  // ★旋转方向冻结(ai3)同骷髅王 35:原版 rotation += direction*0.3 用 TargetClosest\n  //  持久 direction,非每帧 sign(vx)(曾 facing 致贴脸旋冲 spin 反复换号成摆动)\n  if (game.world.clock.isDay && e.ai1 !== 3 && e.ai1 !== 2) {\n    e.ai3 = player && player.cx > e.cx ? 1 : -1;\n    e.ai1 = 2;\n    game.playSfx('roar', 0.8);\n  }\n  if (e.ai1 === 0) {\n    // 悬浮段(:27787-27874):Y 带 [玩家-200, 玩家-500] 0.1/±2;X 窗 ±100 0.1/±8;\n    // mech:Y 带 [玩家-300, 玩家-350]、X 窗平移 -450×侧(:27813-27819)\n    e.ai2 += 1;\n    if (e.ai2 >= 600) {\n      e.ai2 = 0;\n      e.ai1 = 1;\n      e.ai3 = player.cx > e.cx ? 1 : -1;\n    }\n    // 旋转:mech = AngleLerp(vx/15*0.5, 0.75) 阻尼倾转(:27797-27800)/\n    //  非 mech = 裸 vx/15(:27801)\n    if (mq) {\n      const tilt = (e.vx / 15) * 0.5;\n      e.spin += (tilt - e.spin) * 0.75;\n    } else {\n      e.spin = e.vx / 15;\n    }\n    // mech 悬浮带参数(:27813-27819):num516=玩家在头哪侧,num515=-450×侧\n    const yLow = mq ? 300 : 200, yHigh = mq ? 350 : 500;\n    const xShift = mq ? -450 * (player.cx < e.cx ? -1 : 1) : 0;\n    if (e.y > player.y - yLow) {\n      if (e.vy > 0) e.vy *= 0.98;\n      e.vy -= 0.1;\n      if (e.vy > 2) e.vy = 2;\n    } else if (e.y < player.y - yHigh) {\n      if (e.vy < 0) e.vy *= 0.98;\n      e.vy += 0.1;\n      if (e.vy < -2) e.vy = -2;\n    }\n    if (e.cx > player.cx + 100 + xShift) {\n      if (e.vx > 0) e.vx *= 0.98;\n      e.vx -= 0.1;\n      if (e.vx > 8) e.vx = 8;\n    }\n    if (e.cx < player.cx - 100 + xShift) {\n      if (e.vx < 0) e.vx *= 0.98;\n      e.vx += 0.1;\n      if (e.vx < -8) e.vx = -8;\n    }\n  } else if (e.ai1 === 1) {\n    // 旋冲段(:27875-27970):防伤×2,经典 2 速直冲,400t 回悬浮;\n    // mech:旋转改 AngleLerp 倾转(:27887-27892)、冲速 ×0.75(无 135)/0.6\n    // (有 135)(:27951-27955)、距玩家 < 冲速时速度钳到距离防过冲抖动(:27958-27966)\n    e.def.defense = b.def * 2;\n    e.def.damage = b.dmg * 2;\n    e.ai2 += 1;\n    if (e.ai2 === 2) game.playSfx('roar', 0.6);\n    if (e.ai2 >= 400) { e.ai2 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; }\n    if (mq) {\n      const tilt = (e.vx / 15) * 0.5;\n      e.spin += (tilt - e.spin) * 0.75;\n    } else {\n      e.spin += (e.ai3 || 1) * 0.3;\n    }\n    const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;\n    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;\n    let spd = 2;\n    if (mq) {\n      spd *= (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).some((n) => !n.dead && n.vanillaId === 135) ? 0.6 : 0.75;\n    }\n    e.vx = dx / dist * spd; e.vy = dy / dist * spd;\n    if (mq) {\n      // :27958-27966 近距防过冲:距 <0.1 归零,距 < 冲速 → 速度缩到距离\n      const d2 = dist < 0.1 ? 0 : dist;\n      if (d2 < spd) {\n        const vlen = Math.hypot(e.vx, e.vy);\n        if (vlen > 0) { e.vx = e.vx / vlen * d2; e.vy = e.vy / vlen * d2; }\n        else { e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0; }\n      }\n    }\n  } else if (e.ai1 === 2) {\n    // 白天狂暴(:27971-28004):伤/防 9999,速 10+dist/100 钳 [8,32];\n    // mech 旋转改 AngleLerp 倾转(:27950-27954)\n    e.def.damage = 9999;\n    e.def.defense = 9999;\n    if (mq) {\n      const tilt = (e.vx / 15) * 0.5;\n      e.spin += (tilt - e.spin) * 0.75;\n    } else {\n      e.spin += (e.ai3 || 1) * 0.3;\n    }\n    const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;\n    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;\n    let spd = 10 + d / 100;\n    spd = Math.max(8, Math.min(32, spd));\n    e.vx = dx / d * spd; e.vy = dy / d * spd;\n  } else {\n    // 离场(:28043-28052):下坠离场\n    e.vy += 0.1;\n    if (e.vy < 0) e.vy *= 0.95;\n    e.vx *= 0.95;\n    if (e.vy > 13) e.vy = 13;\n    e.encourageDespawn(10); // 离屏即消(bossFled,不播击败)\n  }\n  e.facing = e.vx > 0 ?   }
}

      const pl = Math.hypot(px, py);
      if (pl < 120) {
        this.x = player.cx - (px / (pl || 1)) * 120 - this.w / 2;
        this.y = player.cy - (py / (pl || 1)) * 120 - this.h / 2;
      }
    }
  }
}
```

Hmm wait — placement: vanilla :51206/:51225 blocks run AFTER the movement + rotation — they modify velocity/position post-hoc. Our swarmerAI: sx/sy & dist are computed earlier in scope ✓ (variables available). Where exactly to insert? After the bounce/integration section and before/after wet-dust — vanilla order: movement → rotation → collision bounce → wet → hornet stingers → probe boost → probe kite. I'll insert after the wet/dust, before the light block. But note: our `dist` variable = quantized target distance — vanilla num7 (dist before normalize) = same ✓. But careful — the boost block's `num4` = expected vx (sx here ✓).

Also note the kite block sets base.Center = center - dir*120 where dir = (center - Center).normalize... vanilla: v2 = playerCenter - probeCenter; if |v2| < 120 → probe.Center = playerCenter - v2.normalized*120. My formula: probe center = player.center - (px,py)/pl * 120 where (px,py) = player - probe → probe pushed to 120px away on the same line ✓.

But hmm — the kite applies to detached probes with ai2==0 — for mechdusa-spawned probes ai2 = 134's id (nonzero) → skip. Only probes detached after 134 died (ai3=0, ai2 unchanged — still nonzero!) — hmm vanilla kite condition is ai[2]==0 — probes from mechdusa keep ai2 = head id ≠ 0 → kite never applies to them. Only body-spawned probes (ai2=0). Our destroyer body doesn't spawn probes (vanilla does at :90266 — out of scope; the 2 attached probes suffice). Fine — keep the block for fidelity anyway.

For the attached probe shooting inside mechProbeLock — cadence/aim identical; just call the same helper. Let me design mechProbeLock to do: lock + shooting + tilt + integration, return true. Then the detached shooting block lives in swarmerAI. To avoid duplication, put the shooting logic in a shared exported function `probeFireTick(e, game, player, sx, sy)` in bossAI.ts. For the attached call, sx/sy unused (mech aim path). Simpler: mechProbeLock handles its own shooting (attached always mech → predictive path); swarmerAI detached block handles cadence+aim (uses sx/sy when !mech; predictive when mech — but detached-in-mech-world probes: mechQueenUp true → predictive ✓).

null;\n  if (!host) {\n    host = ens.find((n) => !n.dead && n.vanillaId === 134) ?? null;\n    if (host) e.ai2 = host.id;\n  }\n  if (!host) {\n    e.dontTakeDamage = false;\n    e.ai3 = 0;\n    return false;\n  }\n  // :50898-50907 锁位:绕头公转(半径 26、相位随头 rotation),速度 = queen 速度\n  const ang = host.spin;\n  const ox = 26 * e.ai3;\n  const px = host.cx + ox * Math.cos(ang);\n  const py = host.cy + ox * Math.sin(ang);\n  e.x = px - e.w / 2;\n  e.y = py - e.h / 2;\n  e.vx = queen.vx;\n  e.vy = queen.vy;\n  e.dontTakeDamage = true;\n  // :50975-51007 射击(localAI[0] 挂链 +3/t、≥360 预判弹)\n  e.lai0 += 1 + 2;\n  if (e.justHitT > 0) e.lai0 = 0;\n  if (e.lai0 >= 360) {\n    e.lai0 = 0;\n    if (player && losClear(game, e.cx, e.cy, player.cx, player.cy)) {\n      const dx = player.cx - e.cx - player.vx * 20;\n      const dy = player.cy - e.cy - player.vy * 20;\n      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;\n      shoot(game, e.cx, e.cy, dx / d * 8, dy / d * 8,\n        getAttackDamageForProjectiles(25, 22, e.difficulty), 84);\n    }\n  }\n  // :51041 探针倾斜 rotation = vx×0.1 + 通用积分(锁位点 + queen 速度)\n  e.visAngle = e.vx * 0.1;\n  e.x += e.vx;\n  e.y += e.vy;\n  return true;\n}\n\n/** Mechdusa queen 锚点(NPC.cs:26571-26579 雷眼 / :27194-27202 魔眼)："

1) * e.vanillaScale;\n    const ax = other.cx, ay = other.y + other.h - other.h * 0.5 * scale;\n    let px = e.cx, py = e.y + e.h - e.h * 0.5 * scale;\n    let dx = ax - px, dy = ay - py;\n    if (Math.hypot(dx, dy) > 2000) return;          // :22196-22199\n    const rot = Math.atan2(dy, dx) - Math.PI / 2;\n    const step = 28 * scale, stop = 40 * scale;     // num6\n    let guard = 0;\n    while (guard++ < 200) {\n      const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);\n      if (dist < stop) break;                       // :22204-22208 无尾段\n      const k = step / dist;\n      px += dx * k; py += dy * k;\n      dx = ax - px; dy = ay - py;\n      const ctx = this.ctx;\n      ctx.save();\n      ctx.translate(px, py);\n      ctx.rotate(rot);\n      ctx.drawImage(chain.img, chain.sx, chain.sy, chain.sw, chain.sh,\n        -chain.sw / 2, -chain.sh / 2, chain.sw * scale, chain.sh * scale);\n      ctx.restore();\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** Mechdusa 蛇发（Main.cs:25070-25112，queen 127 绘制段 1:1）：把双子\n   *  125/126 当蛇头——从 queen 头部锚点（GetMechQueenCenter + (∓18/20,-18)\n   *  .RotatedBy(queen.rotation)）向各眼中心铺 Chain12 链；步长 = Chain12 高×\n   *  眼 scale,距目标 <40×scale 停;每段先画后进位（:25097 先 draw 后 +=,与\n   *  双子互连链的先进位后画相反）;origin=(W/2,0) 顶锚。光照着色以无光表\n   *  近似（与 drawTwinsChain 同策略,Lighting.GetColor 合成在全屏光照层）。 */\n  private drawMechdusaHair(e: Enemy, entities: unknown[]) {\n    if (!this.atlas) return;\n    const chain = this.atlas.vmisc('vanilla/Chain12.png');\n    if (!chain) return;\n    const ctx = this.ctx;\n    const qc = getMechQueenCenter(e);\n    for (const twin of entities as Enemy[]) {\n      if (!(twin instanceof Enemy) || twin.dead || (twin.vanillaId !== 125 && twin.vanillaId !== 126)) continue;\n      const scale = (twin.vanilla?.scale ?? 1) * twin.vanillaScale;\n      const tx = twin.cx, ty = twin.cy;\n      // :25081-25084 锚点偏移 (125:-18 / 126:20, -18) 随 queen 旋转\n      const ox = (twin.vanillaId === 125 ? -18 : 20), oy = -18;\n      const cos = Math.cos(e.spin), sin = Math.sin(e.spin);\n      let px = qc.x + ox * cos - oy * sin;\n      let py = qc.y + ox * sin + oy * cos;\n      let dx = tx - px, dy = ty - py;\n      if (Math.hypot(dx, dy) > 2000) continue;      // :25091-25094 flag11\n      const rot = Math.atan2(dy, dx) - Math.PI / 2; // rotation10\n      const stop = 40 * scale;\n      let guard = 0;\n      while (guard++ < 200) {\n        const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);\n        if (dist < stop) break;                     // :25095-25099\n        const k = chain.sh * scale / dist;          // 步长 = Chain12 高×scale\n        dx *= k; dy *= k;\n        ctx.save();\n        ctx.translate(px, py);\n        ctx.rotate(rot);\n        // origin = (Chain12 宽/2, 0)——顶锚（:25101,与双子链的中心锚不同）\n        ctx.drawImage(chain.img, chain.sx, chain.sy, chain.sw, chain.sh,\n          -chain.sw / 2, 0, chain.sw * scale, chain.sh * scale);\n        ctx.restore();\n        px += dx; py += dy;                         // :25102-25103 先画后进位\n        dx = tx - px; dy = ty - py;\n      }\n    }\n  }"
}

=== summonMechdusa ===
  }

  /** 机械美杜莎链召唤（NPC.SpawnMechQueen :19724-19750，奥库瑞姆剃刀 5334 专用）：
   *  mechQueen=-2 标记 → SpawnOnPlayer(127)（SpawnBoss :81469-81473 走 Start=100 专属
   *  分支 + LegacyMisc[107] "机械美杜莎已苏醒!"公告）→ 以其 Center 同点 NewNPC
   *  125/126/134（Start=1）+ 2×139 探针（ai0=毁灭者 whoAmI ±1）。
   *  调用方已过双门（无机械 Boss 在场 + Mechdusa 种子）。返回是否落位成功 */
  private summonMechdusa(): boolean {
    if (this.net && !this.net.policy.isHost) {
      this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.BossHostOnly'));
      return false;
    }
    const st = this.world.store;
    const side = Math.random() < 0.5 ? -1 : 1;
    const tx = Math.max(2, Math.min(st.w - 3, Math.floor((this.player.cx + side * 55 * TILE) / TILE)));
    let ty = 10;
    for (; ty < this.world.h; ty++) {
      if (st.type[st.idx(tx, ty)] !== 0) break;
    }
    const spawnY = Math.max(4, ty - 12) * TILE;
    const prime = Enemy.fromVanilla(127, tx * TILE + 8, spawnY);
    if (!prime) return false;
    this.entities.add(prime, 'enemies');
    this.boss = prime;
    // 同点伴随（SpawnMechQueen :19743-19747：取 Prime Center 为共同出生点；
    // ai0=1 为 Start 参数，探针 ai0=毁灭者引用——本仓以同点落位近似）
    const ccx = prime.cx, ccy = prime.cy;
    for (const nid of [125, 126, 134, 139, 139]) {
      const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(nid, ccx, ccy);
      if (e) { e.id = this.entities.nextId++; this.entities.add(e, 'enemies'); }
    }
    // 公告（SpawnBoss :81492-81496：127 且 mechQueen 命中 → misc[107]，非 HasAwoken）
    if (this.cb.onChat) this.cb.onChat(Lang.misc(107), 175, 75, 255);
    else this.cb.onToast(Lang.misc(107));
    return true;
  }

  /** Boss 同型家族表（"任意 Boss 活跃"门 → 同型门，审计 2026-08-13）：
   *  EoW {13,14,15} / BoC {266,267}——同型在场才拦（原版 Worm Food/Bloody Spine
   *  对已活 Boss 照召 SpawnOnPlayer :43107-43208 无门；本仓单 Boss 槽，
   *  跨型召唤放行、同型拦下防双刷）；未列出的 boss key 维持原"任意 Boss"门 */

```

```
    const st = game.world.store;
    if (!canHit(st, this.cx, this.cy, 1, 1, tgt.cx, tgt.cy, 1, 1)) return;
    const spit = Enemy.fromVanilla(666, this.cx + this.vx, this.cy + this.vy);
    if (!spit) return;
    spit.ai0 = 0; spit.ai1 = 1;
    addEnemy(game, spit);
  }

  /** 由头生成段链（原版各 worm 的 NewNPC 链，NPC.cs:18174+）：body×n + tail */
  static spawnWormChain(head: Enemy, segCount: number): Enemy[] {
    const segs: Enemy[] = [];
    const bodyId = head.vanillaId! + 1, tailId = head.vanillaId! + 2;
    // EoW 族记链总段数（血条恒定分母,EaterOfWorldsProgressBar :30-33;
    // 毁灭者族不消费,统一写入无副作用）
    const chainTotal = segCount + 1;
    head.eowChainMax = chainTotal;
    let prev = head;
    for (let k = 0; k < segCount; k++) {
      const id = k === segCount - 1 ? tailId : bodyId;
      const s = Enemy.fromVanilla(id, head.cx, head.cy);
      if (!s) continue;
      s.wormFollow = prev;
      prev.wormNext = s;
      prev = s;
      s.eowChainMax = chainTotal;
      segs.push(s);
    }
    return segs;
  }

```

 "content": "// Mechdusa（机械美杜莎）合体 Boss 全链探针（NPC.cs 1.4.5.6）：\n//   召唤门/同点链生成/mechQueen 登记（SpawnMechQueen :19724-19751）——\n//   双子 mech 锚位（:26571-26579/:27194-27202 RotatedBy 旋转锚）——\n//   queen 死后回落（IsMechQueenUp :6784-6798 惰性失效）——\n//   毁灭者头锁位+蛇发段链压缩（AI_037 :50611-50620/:50131-50413）——\n//   探针挂链公转（AI_005 :50866-50920）——queen FindFrame 帧 3-5（:75196-75231）——\n//   Prime mech 旋冲减速与离场级联（:27887-27966/:27975-28011）。\nimport { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';\nimport { Enemy } from '../src/entities/Enemy';\nimport { World } from '../src/world/World';\nimport { TILE } from '../src/core/constants';\nimport type { GameHooks } from '../src/entities/types';\nimport type { Player } from '../src/entities/Player';\nimport {\n  spawnMechQueenEnsemble, twinsAI, mechQueenAnchor, skeletronPrimeAI,\n} from '../src/entities/bossAI';\nimport {\n  resetMechQueen, mechQueenUp, getMechQueen, anyMechBossUp, markMechQueen,\n} from '../src/entities/mechQueen';\nimport { vanillaFrameIdx } from '../src/render/Renderer';\nimport { Lang } from '../src/i18n/Lang';\n\nconst W = 200, H = 140, FLOOR = 110;\n\nfunction makeWorld(): World {\n  const w = new World(W, H, 7, 'mechdusa-test');\n  w.clock.timeOfDay = 0;   // 深夜（Clock 默认 8:15AM 白天——白天双子/毁灭者走离场分支）\n  const st = w.store;\n  w.groundLevel = 40;\n  w.rockLevel = 60;\n  for (let x = 0; x < W; x++) st.setTile(x, FLOOR, 1);\n  return w;\n}\n\nfunction makeHooks(world: World, playerAt: { x: number; y: number }) {\n  const enemies: Enemy[] = [];\n  const projectiles: Array<{ projId?: number; damage?: number; vx: number; vy: number }> = [];\n  const em = {\n    nextId: 1,\n    add: (e: unknown, bucket: 'enemies' | 'projectiles') => {\n      const ent = e as { id: number };\n      ent.id = em.nextId++;\n      if (bucket === 'enemies') enemies.push(e as Enemy);\n      else projectiles.push(e as typeof projectiles[number]);\n    },\n  };\n  const px = playerAt.x * TILE, py = playerAt.y * TILE;\n  const player = {\n    x: px, y: py, w: 20, h: 42, cx: px + 10, cy: py + 21,\n    vx: 0, vy: 0, hp: 400, maxHp: 400, dead: false, useTime: 1,\n  } as unknown as Player;\n  const hooks = {\n    world, player,\n    enemies: () => enemies,\n    critters: () => [],\n    spawnDrop: () => null,\n    damagePlayer: () => {},\n    addDamageNumber: () => {},\n    cutTile: () => {},\n    onEnemyKilled: () => {},\n    spawnEnemy: () => {},\n    spawnParticles: () => {},\n    notifyInventoryChanged: () => {},\n    playSfx: () => {},\n    playSfxFiles: () => {},\n    showPickupLabel: () => {},\n    entities: em,\n  } as unknown as GameHooks;\n  return { hooks, enemies, projectiles, em, player };\n}\n\n/** 全员存活敌怪逐 tick 驱动 */\nfunction tick(h: ReturnType<typeof makeHooks>, n: number, only?: Enemy[]) {\n  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {\n    for (const e of only ?? h.enemies) {\n      if (!e.dead) e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, h.hooks);\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nconst CX = 100 * TILE + 8, CY = (FLOOR - 20) * TILE;\n\n/** 起一套完整合体（queen+双子+毁灭者+2 探针） */\nfunction setupEnsemble(playerAt = { x: 100, y: 100 }) {\n  const h = makeHooks(makeWorld(), playerAt);\n  const queen = spawnMechQueenEnsemble(h.hooks, CX, CY)!;\n  return { h, queen };\n}\n\nbeforeEach(() => resetMechQueen());\n\ndescribe('召唤门与链生成（NPC.SpawnMechQueen :19724-19751）', () => {\n  it('anyMechBossUp：空场 false；127/125/126/134 任一存活 true；死体不计', () => {\n    const h = makeHooks(makeWorld(), { x: 100, y: 100 });\n    expect(anyMechBossUp(h.enemies)).toBe(false);\n    for (const id of [127, 125, 126, 134]) {\n      const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(id, CX, CY)!;\n      e.id = h.em.nextId++;\n      h.enemies.push(e);\n      expect(anyMechBossUp(h.enemies), `id=${id}`).toBe(true);\n      e.dead = true;\n      expect(anyMechBossUp(h.enemies), `id=${id} dead`).toBe(false);\n    }\n  });\n\n  it('同点生成：127+125+126+134+2×139 共 6 实体,全部落在 queen Center', () => {\n    const { h, queen } = setupEnsemble();\n    expect(h.enemies).toHaveLength(6);\n    const ids = h.enemies.map((e) => e.vanillaId).sort((a, b) => (a ?? 0) - (b ?? 0));\n    expect(ids).toEqual([125, 126, 127, 134, 139, 139]);\n    for (const e of h.enemies) {\n      expect(Math.abs(e.cx - queen.cx)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);   // (int)Center 同点\n      expect(Math.abs(e.cy - queen.cy)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);\n    }\n  });\n\n  it('探针挂链参数：ai2=毁灭者头引用、ai3=∓1 两侧（:19746-19747 第 7/8 参）', () => {\n    const { h } = setupEnsemble();\n    const head = h.enemies.find((e) => e.vanillaId === 134)!;\n    const probes = h.enemies.filter((e) => e.vanillaId === 139);\n    expect(probes).toHaveLength(2);\n    expect(probes.map((p) => p.ai3).sort()).toEqual([-1, 1]);\n    for (const p of probes) expect(p.ai2).toBe(head.id);\n  });\n\n  it('mechQueen 登记：召唤即刻 mechQueenUp/getMechQueen 命中（:19742）', () => {\n    const { queen } = setupEnsemble();\n    expect(mechQueenUp()).toBe(true);\n    expect(getMechQueen()).toBe(queen);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('登记失效与复位（IsMechQueenUp :6784-6798 / clearWorld :6907）', () => {\n  it('queen 死亡 → mechQueenUp 惰性 false + 复位', () => {\n    const { queen } = setupEnsemble();\n    queen.dead = true;\n    expect(mechQueenUp()).toBe(false);\n    expect(getMechQueen()).toBeNull();\n    // 复活不影响（已复位,须重登记）\n    queen.dead = false;\n    expect(mechQueenUp()).toBe(false);\n  });\n\n  it('resetMechQueen 清登记（进世界复位语义）', () => {\n    setupEnsemble();\n    resetMechQueen();\n    expect(mechQueenUp()).toBe(false);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('双子 mech 锚位（NPC.cs:26571-26579 雷眼 / :27194-27202 魔眼）', () => {\n  it('锚点 = GetMechQueenCenter + (±150,-250)×0.75 绕中心 RotatedBy(queen.vx×0.025)', () => {\n    const { queen } = setupEnsemble();\n    queen.x = 1000 - queen.w / 2; queen.y = 500 - queen.h / 2; queen.vx = 0;\n    const ret = mechQueenAnchor(queen, false);\n    // vx=0 → 无旋转:中心 (1000,486) + (-112.5,-187.5)\n    expect(ret.x).toBeCloseTo(1000 - 112.5, 5);\n    expect(ret.y).toBeCloseTo(486 - 187.5, 5);\n    const spaz = mechQueenAnchor(queen, true);\n    expect(spaz.x).toBeCloseTo(1000 + 112.5, 5);\n    // queen.vx=20 → 0.5rad 旋转偏移\n    queen.vx = 20;\n    const ang = 0.5;\n    const r = mechQueenAnchor(queen, false);\n    const ox = -112.5, oy = -187.5;\n    expect(r.x).toBeCloseTo(1000 + ox * Math.cos(ang) - oy * Math.sin(ang), 5);\n    expect(r.y).toBeCloseTo(486 + ox * Math.sin(ang) + oy * Math.cos(ang), 5);\n  });\n\n  it('雷眼一阶段:速度朝旋转锚点指数逼近 1/60(:26622-26679,速钳 14)', () => {\n    const { h, queen } = setupEnsemble();\n    queen.x = 1000 - queen.w / 2; queen.y = 500 - queen.h / 2; queen.vx = 20;\n    const twin = h.enemies.find((e) => e.vanillaId === 125)!;\n    // 眼放远处:delta 钳到 14 长度,v=(0×59+delta)/60\n    twin.x = 1000 - twin.w / 2; twin.y = 1000 - twin.h / 2; twin.vx = 0; twin.vy = 0;\n    twinsAI(twin, h.hooks, h.player, false);\n    const a = mechQueenAnchor(queen, false);\n    let dx = a.x - twin.cx, dy = a.y - twin.cy;\n    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);\n    expect(d).toBeGreaterThan(14);\n    dx = dx / d * 14; dy = dy / d * 14;\n    expect(twin.vx).toBeCloseTo(dx / 60, 5);\n    expect(twin.vy).toBeCloseTo(dy / 60, 5);\n    expect(Math.hypot(twin.vx, twin.vy)).toBeCloseTo(14 / 60, 5);\n  });\n\n  it('魔眼一阶段:同锚点但逼近系数 1/5(:27241-27248)', () => {\n    const { h, queen } = setupEnsemble();\n    queen.x = 1000 - queen.w / 2; queen.y = 500 - queen.h / 2; queen.vx = 0;\n    const twin = h.enemies.find((e) => e.vanillaId === 126)!;\n    twin.x = 1400 - twin.w / 2; twin.y = 900 - twin.h / 2; twin.vx = 0; twin.vy = 0;\n    twinsAI(twin, h.hooks, h.player, true);\n    const a = mechQueenAnchor(queen, true);\n    let dx = a.x - twin.cx, dy = a.y - twin.cy;\n    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);\n    dx = dx / d * 14; dy = dy / d * 14;\n    expect(twin.vx).toBeCloseTo((0 * 4 + dx) / 5, 5);\n    expect(twin.vy).toBeCloseTo((0 * 4 + dy) / 5, 5);\n  });\n\n  it('queen 死后回落:速度不再朝锚点,改玩家侧悬浮点(:26607-26620 常规档)', () => {\n    const { h, queen } = setupEnsemble();\n    queen.dead = true;\n    expect(mechQueenUp()).toBe(false);\n    const twin = h.enemies.find((e) => e.vanillaId === 125)!;\n    twin.x = (h.player.cx) - twin.w / 2; twin.y = h.player.cy - twin.h / 2;\n    twin.vx = 0; twin.vy = 0;\n    twinsAI(twin, h.hooks, h.player, false);\n    // 常规档 seek:朝玩家侧 300/上 300 悬浮点 accelTo ±0.1 步进\n    const side = twin.cx < h.player.x + h.player.w ? -1 : 1;\n    const tx = h.player.cx + side * 300;\n    expect(Math.sign(twin.vx)).toBe(Math.sign(tx - twin.cx));\n    expect(twin.vx).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.2);\n  });\n\n  it('变身段 mech 反弹弹幕旗标(:26812/:27435),非 mech 复位', () => {\n    const { h, queen } = setupEnsemble();\n    const twin = h.enemies.find((e) => e.vanillaId === 125)!;\n    twin.ai0 = 1; twin.ai1 = 0; twin.ai2 = 0; twin.vx = 0; twin.vy = 0;\n    twinsAI(twin, h.hooks, h.player, false);\n    expect(twin.reflectsProjectiles).toBe(true);\n    queen.dead = true;\n    twinsAI(twin, h.hooks, h.player, false);\n    expect(twin.reflectsProjectiles).toBe(false);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('Prime 头 mech 分支（AI_032 :27736-28054）', () => {\n  it('旋冲减速:冲速 2×0.6(有 135 段在场)=1.2(:27951-27955)', () => {\n    const { h, queen } = setupEnsemble();\n    // 毁灭者已生成 135 段链(destroyerAI 首帧)——先驱动毁灭者一帧出链\n    const dest = h.enemies.find((e) => e.vanillaId === 134)!;\n    tick(h, 1, [dest]);\n    expect(h.enemies.some((e) => !e.dead && e.vanillaId === 135)).toBe(true);\n    queen.ai1 = 1; queen.ai2 = 0;\n    skeletronPrimeAI(queen, h.hooks, h.player);\n    const spd = Math.hypot(queen.vx, queen.vy);\n    expect(spd).toBeCloseTo(2 * 0.6, 3);\n    // 段全灭 → ×0.75 = 1.5\n    for (const e of h.enemies) if (e.vanillaId === 135 || e.vanillaId === 136) e.dead = true;\n    skeletronPrimeAI(queen, h.hooks, h.player);\n    expect(Math.hypot(queen.vx, queen.vy)).toBeCloseTo(2 * 0.75, 3);\n  });\n\n  it('离场级联:双子尽 → 毁灭者头静默消散 + 自身鼓励消散(:27975-28011)', () => {\n    const { h, queen } = setupEnsemble();\n    for (const e of h.enemies) {\n      if (e.vanillaId === 125 || e.vanillaId === 126) e.dead = true;\n    }\n    const dest = h.enemies.find((e) => e.vanillaId === 134)!;\n    queen.ai1 = 3;\n    skeletronPrimeAI(queen, h.hooks, h.player);\n    expect(dest.dead).toBe(true);          // Transform(136) → 段死分支消散\n    expect(dest.bossFled).toBe(true);      // 静默:不走击败记账\n    expect(queen.despawnEncouraged).toBe(true);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('毁灭者 mech 锁位与蛇发段链（AI_037 :50611-50620/:50131-50413）', () => {\n  it('头锁位:中心 = queen 锚 + (0,100) 旋转 + queen 速度,速度清零,旋转 = ang×0.75+π', () => {\n    const { h, queen } = setupEnsemble();\n    queen.x = 2000 - queen.w / 2; queen.y = 800 - queen.h / 2;\n    queen.vx = 20; queen.vy = 0;\n    const dest = h.enemies.find((e) => e.vanillaId === 134)!;\n    tick(h, 1, [dest]);\n    const qc = { x: 2000, y: 800 - 14 };            // GetMechQueenCenter\n    const ang = 20 * 0.025;\n    const px = qc.x - 100 * Math.sin(ang), py = qc.y + 100 * Math.cos(ang);\n    expect(dest.cx).toBeCloseTo(px + 20, 4);\n    expect(dest.cy).toBeCloseTo(py, 4);\n    expect(dest.vx).toBe(0);\n    expect(dest.vy).toBe(0);\n    expect(dest.spin).toBeCloseTo(ang * 0.75 + Math.PI, 5);\n  });\n\n  it('蛇发段链压缩:头后第 1 段跟距 4.4px、Y 悬挂 44(:50386-50410);≥10 段回落 44 常规', () => {\n    const { h, queen } = setupEnsemble();\n    const dest = h.enemies.find((e) => e.vanillaId === 134)!;\n    tick(h, 1, [dest]);                              // 出链 + 头锁位\n    const s1 = dest.wormNext!;\n    // 放到头右侧 200px:目标向量 = (头-段, 头+44-段),收缩到 4.4\n    s1.x = dest.cx + 200 - s1.w / 2;\n    s1.y = dest.cy - s1.h / 2;\n    tick(h, 1, [dest]);\n    const targetY = dest.cy + 44;                    // num25(num=1)=44\n    const dist = Math.hypot(s1.cx - dest.cx, s1.cy - targetY);\n    expect(dist).toBeCloseTo(4.4, 1);                // num24/10×num = 4.4\n    // 第 11 段(idx=11 ≥ 10 → num=0)走常规 44px 跟随(无 Y 悬挂偏置)\n    let seg: Enemy | null = dest.wormNext;\n    for (let i = 1; i < 11 && seg; i++) seg = seg.wormNext;\n    expect(seg).not.toBeNull();\n    const s11 = seg!;\n    s11.x = dest.cx + 300 - s11.w / 2;\n    s11.y = dest.cy - s11.h / 2;\n    tick(h, 1, [dest]);\n    const d11 = Math.hypot(s11.cx - dest.cx, s11.cy - dest.cy);\n    expect(d11).toBeCloseTo(44, 1);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('探针挂链公转（AI_005 :50866-50920）', () => {\n  it('ai3≠0 探针绕毁灭者头公转锁位 + 无敌;速度 = queen 速度', () => {\n    const { h, queen } = setupEnsemble();\n    queen.vx = 0; queen.vy = 0;\n    const dest = h.enemies.find((e) => e.vanillaId === 134)!;\n    tick(h, 1, [dest]);                              // 头锁位(spin = 0×0.75+π)\n    const probes = h.enemies.filter((e) => e.vanillaId === 139);\n    const p = probes[0];\n    tick(h, 1, [p]);\n    // spin=π:offset (26×ai3,0) 旋转 π → (-26×ai3,0)\n    const expectedX = dest.cx - 26 * p.ai3;\n    expect(p.cx).toBeCloseTo(expectedX, 3);\n    expect(p.cy).toBeCloseTo(dest.cy, 3);\n    expect(p.dontTakeDamage).toBe(true);\n    // 134 死 → 探针脱落回常规(ai3=0,解除无敌)\n    dest.dead = true;\n    tick(h, 1, [p]);\n    expect(p.ai3).toBe(0);\n    expect(p.dontTakeDamage).toBe(false);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('queen FindFrame 帧 3-5（NPC.cs:75196-75231）', () => {\n  it('mech 态:悬浮 0..4 爬坡后 3↔4 循环;攻击态恒帧 5;非 mech 保持 0-2', () => {\n    const { queen } = setupEnsemble();\n    expect(mechQueenUp()).toBe(true);\n    queen.ai1 = 0;\n    const seq: number[] = [];\n    for (let t = 0; t <= 8 * 12; t += 12) {\n      queen.animT = t;\n      seq.push(vanillaFrameIdx(queen, 6));\n    }\n    expect(seq).toEqual([0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 3, 4, 3, 4]);\n    queen.ai1 = 1;\n    expect(vanillaFrameIdx(queen, 6)).toBe(5);\n    resetMechQueen();                                // 非 mech 回落\n    queen.ai1 = 0; queen.animT = 13;\n    expect(vanillaFrameIdx(queen, 6)).toBe(1);\n    queen.ai1 = 1;\n    expect(vanillaFrameIdx(queen, 6)).toBe(2);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('公告 LegacyMisc[107]（SpawnBoss :81492-81496）', () => {\n  it('Lang.misc(107) = \"机械美杜莎已苏醒!\"（原版键,无自有 l10n 键）', () => {\n    expect(Lang.misc(107)).toContain('机械美杜莎');\n  });\n});\n"
}

export function spawnMechQueenEnsemble(game: GameHooks, cx: number, cy: number): Enemy | null {
  const queen = spawnVanillaEnemy(game, 127, cx, cy);
  if (!queen) return null;
  // SpawnBoss :81469-81473：Type==127 且 mechQueen!=-1 → ai[3]=whoAmI(非零标记)；
  // 本仓 ai3 是旋冲方向缓存(±1)不冲突——mech 态旋转走 AngleLerp 不读 ai3,标记走 WeakSet
  markMechQueen(queen);
  queen.ai3 = queen.id;
  // :19743-19747 同点伴随(取 mechQueen 的 Center);探针 ai2=134 引用、ai3=∓1 侧位
  for (const id of [125, 126]) spawnVanillaEnemy(game, id, queen.cx, queen.cy);
  const head = spawnVanillaEnemy(game, 134, queen.cx, queen.cy);
  for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
    const probe = spawnVanillaEnemy(game, 139, queen.cx, queen.cy);
    if (probe) { probe.ai2 = head ? head.id : -1; probe.ai3 = side; }
  }
  return queen;
}

/** Mechdusa 探针 139 挂链锁位(NPC.cs AI_005 :50866-50920 + :50975-51007)：
 *  ai3≠0 = 召唤链出生的挂链探针——queen 失效/头失效 → 脱落(ai3=0 回常规 swarmer,
 *  dontTakeDamage 解除);头在场 → 锁位绕头(头中心 + (26×ai3,0).RotatedBy(头 rotation,
 *  头中心)),速度 = queen 速度,无敌;localAI[0] 每 tick +3、受击清零,≥360 发 84 号
 *  弹(25 伤,玩家速度×20 预判 8 速,Projectile.cs 命中端另乘难度)。
 *  返回 true = 已锁位并完成积分(调用方须跳过常规移动段)。 */
export function mechProbeLock(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null): boolean {
  if (!mechQueenUp()) {
    e.dontTakeDamage = false;
    e.ai3 = 0;                        // :50909-50919 else 分支:脱落
    return false;
  }
  const queen = getMechQueen()!;
  // :50877-50881 ai2 引用失效(头槽变号) → FindFirstNPC(134) 重挂
  const ens = game.enemies() as Enemy[];
  let host: Enemy | null = ens.find((n) => !n.dead && n.vanillaId === 134 && n.id === e.ai2) ?? null;
  if (!host) {
    host = ens.find((n) => !n.dead && n.vanillaId === 134) ?? null;
    if (host) e.ai2 = host.id;
  }
  if (!host) {
    e.dontTakeDamage = false;
    e.ai3 = 0;
    return false;
  }
  // :50898-50907 锁位:绕头公转(半径 26、相位随头 rotation),速度 = queen 速度
  const ang = host.spin;
  const ox = 26 * e.ai3;
  const px = host.cx + ox * Math.cos(ang);
  const py = host.cy + ox * Math.sin(ang);
  e.x = px - e.w / 2;
  e.y = py - e.h / 2;
  e.vx = queen.vx;
  e.vy = queen.vy;
  e.dontTakeDamage = true;
  // :50975-51007 射击(localAI[0] 挂链 +3/t、≥360 预判弹)
  e.lai0 += 1 + 2;
  if (e.justHitT > 0) e.lai0 = 0;
  if (e.lai0 >= 360) {
    e.lai0 = 0;
    if (player && losClear(game, e.cx, e.cy, player.cx, player.cy)) {
      const dx = player.cx - e.cx - player.vx * 20;
      const dy = player.cy - e.cy - player.vy * 20;
      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
      shoot(game, e.cx, e.cy, dx / d * 8, dy / d * 8,
        getAttackDamageForProjectiles(25, 22, e.difficulty), 84);
    }
  }
  // :51041 探针倾斜 rotation = vx×0.1 + 通用积分(锁位点 + queen 速度)
  e.visAngle = e.vx * 0.1;
  e.x += e.vx;
  e.y += e.vy;
  return true;
}

/** Mechdusa queen 锚点(NPC.cs:26571-26579 雷眼 / :27194-27202 魔眼)：
 *  GetMechQueenCenter + (±150,-250)×0.75 再 RotatedBy(queen.velocity.X×0.05×0.5, 中心)
 *  ——双子挂 queen 头上,随 queen 水平速度整体旋转。返回绝对坐标。 */
export function mechQueenAnchor(queen: Enemy, spaz: boolean): { x: number; y: number } {
  const qc = getMechQueenCenter(queen);
  // vector38/44 = new Vector2(±150, -250) × 0.75
  const ox = (spaz ? 150 : -150) * 0.75, oy = -250 * 0.75;
  const ang = queen.vx * 0.025;            // num431/num475
  const cos = Math.cos(ang), sin = Math.sin(ang);
  // RotatedBy(ang, qc)：绕 qc 旋转 (qc+off) 点
  return { x: qc.x + ox * cos - oy * sin, y: qc.y + ox * sin + oy * cos };
}

/** AI_030/031 双子(NPC.cs:26497-27735):雷神之眼 125 / 魔神之眼 126。
 *  旋转持续瞄准玩家(0.1/0.15 步进+绕圈处理);白天/玩家死 → 上飞离场;
 *  一阶段(血>40%):侧向悬浮(300/400)→4-10 连冲(12/13 速);二阶段变身自旋;
 *  三阶段:雷眼 激光 83→100(伤害×1.5 防+10),魔眼 火焰喷射 101(伤害×1.5 防+18)。
 *  getGoodWorld(FTW)分支全量实装:悬浮/冲刺/弹幕计数抬档(:26599/26709/26745/
 *  26883/27020 雷眼,:27218/27313/27373/27539 魔眼)。
 *  Mechdusa 联动(IsMechQueenUp,2026-08-14 全分支实装):锚位悬浮(:26571-26579/
 *  :27194-27202)、悬浮计时 600→1200(:26685/:27291)、雷眼激光门 60→90/120
 *  (:26675-26680,有 135 段在场=120)、变身段反弹弹幕(:26812/:27435)、
 *  三阶段锚位悬浮+计时 300/400→1200(:26891-26906/:27510-27587/:27651-27671)、
 *  魔眼三阶段旋转步进 ×0.25(:27135-27139)、火焰沿自身朝向喷(:27640-27647)。 */
export function twinsAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null, spaz: boolean) {
  if (!e.bInit) { e.bInit = true; e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; }
  // Main.getGoodWorld(for the worthy)
  const ftw = !!game.world.seedFlags?.getGoodWorld;
  // Mechdusa 合体态(NPC.IsMechQueenUp)——queen 失效(死亡/消散)自动回落 false
  const mq = mechQueenUp();
  const queen = mq ? getMechQueen() : null;
  // 白天/玩家死:上飞离场(原版 :26585/:27208 vy-=0.04 + EncourageDespawn(10))——
  // 玩家死亡派发为 null,一并进离场（此前 null 仅漂移不消散）
